      const theme = (() => {
        if (
          typeof localStorage !== "undefined" &&
          localStorage.getItem("theme")
        ) {
          return localStorage.getItem("theme");
        }
        if (window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches) {
          return "dark";
        }
        return "light";
      })();

      if (theme === "light") {
        document.documentElement.classList.remove("dark");
      } else {
        document.documentElement.classList.add("dark");
      }

      window.localStorage.setItem("theme", theme);

      const handleToggleClick = () => {
        const element = document.documentElement;
        element.classList.toggle("dark");

        const isDark = element.classList.contains("dark");
        localStorage.setItem("theme", isDark ? "dark" : "light");
      };
    </script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/docs/_astro/_page_.BFxoJ5Nz.css">

  (() => {
    const script = document.currentScript;
    const cssVariable = script?.getAttribute("data-css-variable");
    const parent = script?.parentElement;
    if (!parent || !cssVariable || !parent) return;
    const updateHeight = () => {
      const height = parent.clientHeight;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVariable, `${height}px`);
    };
    updateHeight();
    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(updateHeight);
    resizeObserver.observe(parent);
  })();
</script> </header>  <div class="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] grid-flow-row"> <div class="sticky top-[var(--header-height)] self-start z-40"> <astro-island uid="1jGuPn" prefix="r4" That means a client can receive multiple updates from a single <!-- -->subscription:</p><div class="mb-6"><div></div></div><p class="mb-3">Subscriptions<!-- --> are best suited to apps that rely on Instead, it receives updates via <a href="https://www.apollographql.com/docs/graphos/routing/operations/subscriptions/multipart-protocol/" class="hover:underline underline hover:opacity-75 font-medium" data-astro-prefetch="viewport">multipart HTTP responses</a>.</p></li><li class="mb-3"><p class="mb-3">By using HTTP for <!-- -->subscriptions,<!-- --> clients can execute all <!-- -->GraphQL<!-- --> <!-- -->operation<!-- --> types over HTTP instead of using two different protocols.</p></li><li class="mb-3"><p class="mb-3"><a href="https://www.apollographql.com/docs/react/data/subscriptions#http" class="hover:underline underline hover:opacity-75 font-medium" data-astro-prefetch="viewport">Apollo Client</a>, <a href="https://www.apollographql.com/docs/kotlin/essentials/subscriptions#configuring-http-subscriptions" class="hover:underline underline hover:opacity-75 font-medium" data-astro-prefetch="viewport">Apollo Kotlin</a>, and <a href="https://www.apollographql.com/docs/ios/fetching/subscriptions#http" class="hover:underline underline hover:opacity-75 font-medium" data-astro-prefetch="viewport">Apollo iOS</a> all support <!-- -->GraphQL<!-- --> <!-- -->subscriptions<!-- --> over HTTP with minimal configuration. See each library's documentation for details. <span class="group underline inline-block relative cursor-help underline-offset-4 decoration-dotted decoration-navy-100 dark:decoration-blue-400">Apollo Client</span> also provides network adapters for the <a href="https://www.apollographql.com/docs/react/data/subscriptions#relay" class="hover:underline underline hover:opacity-75 font-medium" data-astro-prefetch="viewport">Relay</a> and <a href="https://www.apollographql.com/docs/react/data/subscriptions#urql" class="hover:underline underline hover:opacity-75 font-medium" data-astro-prefetch="viewport">urql</a> libraries.</p></li></ul></li><li class="mb-3"><p class="mb-3">When your <!-- -->router<!-- --> receives a <!-- -->subscription,<!-- --> it executes that same <!-- -->subscription<!-- --> against whichever <!-- -->subgraph<!-- --> defines the requested <!-- -->field—<code class="font-mono bg-silver-400 dark:bg-navy-400 px-1 rounded" style="overflow-wrap:normal">stockPricesChanged</code> in the code snippet above.</p><ul class="list-disc ml-4"><li class="mb-3"><p class="mb-3">This communication usually does use a WebSocket subprotocol, for compatibility with most <!-- -->subgraph<!-- --> libraries.</p></li><li class="mb-3"><p class="mb-3">With a <!-- -->self-hosted router,<!-- --> you can also configure an <a href="https://www.apollographql.com/docs/graphos/routing/operations/subscriptions/#http-callback-setup" class="hover:underline underline hover:opacity-75 font-medium" data-astro-prefetch="viewport">HTTP-callback-based protocol</a>.</p></li></ul></li><li class="mb-3"><p class="mb-3">The <!-- -->subgraph<!-- --> periodically sends new data to your <!-- -->router.<!-- --> Whenever it does, the <!-- -->router<!-- --> returns that data to the client in an additional HTTP response part.</p><ul class="list-disc ml-4"><li class="mb-3"><p class="mb-3">A <!-- -->subscription<!-- --> can include federated <span class="group underline inline-block relative cursor-help underline-offset-4 decoration-dotted decoration-navy-100 dark:decoration-blue-400">entity</span> <!-- -->fields<!-- --> that are defined in other <!-- -->subgraphs.<!-- --> If it does, the <!-- -->router<!-- --> first fetches those <!-- -->fields<!-- --> by <!-- -->querying<!-- --> the corresponding <!-- -->subgraphs,<!-- --> such as the <strong class="font-bold">Portfolios <!-- -->subgraph</strong> in the diagram above. These queries use HTTP as usual.</p></li></ul></li></ol><div class="mb-3"><div class="bg-blue-100 dark:bg-navy-450 border-navy-400 border-l-4 p-4 rounded-r-md"><div class="flex items-center mb-2"><span class="flex-shrink-0 pr-2"><div class="w-[var(--icon-size)] h-[var(--icon-size)] text-current fill-none" style="--icon-size:16px"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 16 16"><g fill="none" stroke="currentColor"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9.406 10.813h-.469A.94.94 0 0 1 8 9.875V7.531a.47.47 0 0 0-.469-.468h-.468"></path><path d="M7.766 5.188a.234.234 0 1 1 0-.47M7.766 5.188a.234.234 0 1 0 0-.47"></path><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M8 15.031A7.032 7.032 0 1 0 8 .968a7.032 7.032 0 0 0 0 14.063"></path></g></svg></div></span><strong class="uppercase">note</strong></div><div>Apollo Router<!-- --> does not support direct WebSocket connections from clients.   (() => {
    const script = document.currentScript;
    const cssVariable = script?.getAttribute("data-css-variable");
    const parent = script?.parentElement;
    if (!parent || !cssVariable || !parent) return;
    const updateHeight = () => {
      const height = parent.clientHeight;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVariable, `${height}px`);
    };
    updateHeight();
    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(updateHeight);
    resizeObserver.observe(parent);
  })();
</script></div>  <footer class="bg-gray-800 py-12 w-full justify-end border-t border-primary-base dark:border-primary-dark"> <div class="container mx-auto px-4"> <div class="grid grid-cols-1
